# Commands · P · part 3

1,422 entries, pev-verify to plan. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pev-verify**: Run only the Verify phase. Validates changes against plan.md acceptance criteria ([myksyut/pev-harness](https://github.com/myksyut/pev-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/pev-verify.md) · ★3)

- **pf-cross-review**: Run a contradictory code review on a git range using N0 (Claude/Anthropic sub-agent) + N1 (Codex/OpenAI CLI) + N2 (local LLM endpoint (e.g. llama-swap, vLLM, Ollama): qwen36-27b CN + devstral-small-2 EU). V3.3 — activates N2 via llama-swap HTTP direct (was bus placeholder in V2.1). V3.2 shipped anti-symmetric-inverse repro guards. Use after commits to catch blind spots same-LLM self-review misses. ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/blob/HEAD/extensions/pf-cross-review/commands/cross-review.md))
- **pf-cross-review**: Run a contradictory code review using N0 (Anthropic sub-agent) + N1 (Codex CLI) on a git range, consolidate findings into a structured doc ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/pf-cross-review.md))
- **pf-cross-review-iterate**: Iterative auto-stabilization wrapper around /pf-cross-review. Runs N rounds until convergence (no new findings for K rounds), divergence detection, max iterations, or budget cap. SCAFFOLD-CEGIS safety monotonicity pattern. ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/pf-cross-review-iterate.md))
- **pf-cross-review-iterate**: Wrap /pf-cross-review V3.3 in an iterative auto-stabilization loop. Runs N rounds until convergence (no new findings for K consecutive rounds), divergence detection (new findings &gt; 1.5× previous = spec drift alarm), max iterations cap (5), or budget cap. Implements SCAFFOLD-CEGIS safety monotonicity pattern. Use after a cluster of substantial commits where you suspect fix iterations might introduce regressions. ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/blob/HEAD/extensions/pf-cross-review-iterate/commands/iterate.md))

- **pfadd**: Adds elements to a HyperLogLog key. Creates the key if it doesn't exist. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pfadd.md) · ★86)
- **pfcount**: Returns the approximated cardinality of the set(s) observed by the HyperLogLog key(s). ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pfcount.md) · ★86)
- **pfd-all**: Run all three PFD modes (analyze, solve, evaluate) on the same input. Produces three full outputs side-by-side, one per cognitive contract. Use when a question genuinely warrants multiple cognitive contracts, or when you want to see what the question reveals across descriptive, prescriptive, and rated lenses simultaneously. ([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-all.md))
- **pfd-analyze**: Analyze a hypothetical change, mechanism, or behavioral pattern with Perception-First Design. Mode 3 descriptive analysis. Walks the 5 layers as predictive lenses to enumerate cascading consequences, trade-offs, and integrative compounds. Use for "what happens if", "why does X work", "users say X but do Y" questions that want results, not recommendations. ([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-analyze.md))
- **pfd-cycle**: 計画PFDから次の着手可能プロセスを選んで完了させ、知見をリポジトリ成果物に反映する ([takasek/pfdsl](https://github.com/takasek/pfdsl/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-cycle.md) · 3 copies)
- **pfd-evaluate**: Evaluate an artifact (URL, HTML, screenshot, copy) with Perception-First Design. Mode 1 corpus-backed evaluation that loads heuristic rules, design system profiles, worked examples, and the psychology corpus to produce a layered scoring with citations. ([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-evaluate.md))
- **pfd-init**: 新規プロジェクトに .pfdsl/ を初期構築する ([takasek/pfdsl](https://github.com/takasek/pfdsl/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-init.md) · 3 copies)
- **pfd-retro**: PFDと版に対応した実行証拠を照合し、モデル改訂の要否を判断する ([takasek/pfdsl](https://github.com/takasek/pfdsl/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-retro.md) · 3 copies)
- **pfd-solve**: Solve a design, marketing, writing, or communication problem with Perception-First Design. Runs the Mode 2 derivation protocol — derives requirements R1-R5 from cognitive constraints, then produces a solution that satisfies all of them. ([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-solve.md))
- **pfdebug**: Internal commands for debugging HyperLogLog values. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pfdebug.md) · ★86)
- **pfm**: Operates the fleet CLI — `pfm` verb map, adopter updates (`pfm doctor --project-updates`, `pfm update adopt|pin|ignore|drop|accept`), `pfm install --yes`, `pfm doctor`, chat MCP; load for any `pfm` verb or question. Not for editing framework files → the `pcm` agent. ([rezzminator/professor](https://github.com/rezzminator/professor/blob/HEAD/templates/global/commands/pfm.md) · ★9)
- **pfm-testing-manual**: Governs how pfm (the Go fleet engine) is tested — tiers, test homes, the journey, mocks, run commands, gates and floors, bug classes, traps. Read by the flights foremen for the affected tests, the flights lander whole for the full gate; `/pfm-testing-manual` opens it for a human. Keep it true in the same change that alters how pfm is tested. ([rezzminator/professor](https://github.com/rezzminator/professor/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfm-testing-manual.md) · ★9)

- **pg-health**: Read-only PostgreSQL health check with queryshift, then a prioritized diagnosis ([sarankumar1325/pgbot](https://github.com/sarankumar1325/pgbot/blob/HEAD/commands/pg-health.md))

- **phase-continue**: Continue a paused phase from its phase-N-checkpoint.md in a fresh executor session. Loads the checkpoint, mandatory EnterPlanMode to plan only the PENDING work-steps, executes, writes canonical phase-N-handoff.md with carried-from-checkpoint annotations, renames checkpoint to .bak. Invoked by the orchestrator's 'Re-spawn from checkpoint' menu pick or manually with /phase-continue &lt;run-id&gt; &lt;N&gt;. Triggers: 'continue paused phase', 'resume from checkpoint', 'pick up phase from checkpoint'. ([unifylabs-dev/unify-kit](https://github.com/unifylabs-dev/unify-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/unifylabs-workflow/commands/phase-continue.md))

Interviews for goal-type, trigger signal, report sections, and verdict; synthesizes a phase.json + persona; registers them via `evolve phases create`; self-corrects from the command's JSON envelope. The Go binary is the single enforcement point, so this flow works from any LLM CLI. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/commands/phase-create.md) · ★7)

- **phonology-examples-check**: `phonology-examples check` queues an advisory check of one saved Example Set revision against the grammar in the project's current [Baseline](term:baseline). For each example it asks PanGloss to generate surface forms from the underlying form in the example's declared context and reports whether the expected form was observed. A check never changes the FieldWorks project, never changes Readiness, ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-check.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-list**: `phonology-examples list` reads the current revision of each saved phonology Example Set from the project's Motif store. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-list.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-results**: `phonology-examples results` reads the stored result of one phonology check: a page of per-example summaries, or one complete example. A check is advisory evidence about a saved Example Set against a [Baseline](term:baseline); it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-results.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-save**: `phonology-examples save` records underlying forms, expected surface forms and the grammar context for each example as an immutable revision, with an optional citation on each. A saved set is advisory evidence: it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. Saving does not change the FieldWorks project. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-save.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-show**: `phonology-examples show` reads one exact saved revision and returns its examples in authored order, including source context and any citations. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-show.md) · ★1)

- **pipeline-undo**: Undo a pipeline run's result. With worktree isolation (the current engine), this is clean and low-risk: a run never touches your checkout — its result lives only on a `pipeline/&lt;session&gt;` branch (and, for a `--push` run, on the remote). "Undo" therefore means deleting that branch and its worktree, not reverting your working tree.
